# Household hh_s14 — 32 days, Monday to Thursday

Seed-generated household. Times are clock times; ids are the receptacle and object ids used in events.jsonl. A line indented under an activity says where an object went when the activity ended and why. WHIM marks a placement that landed somewhere other than where the decision was heading. Text in [brackets] names the hidden cause the decision is attributed to.

## Household

Type: couple. Rooms: bathroom, bedroom_1, entry, kitchen, living, office, storage.

Residents:

- **Marco** (resident_1): works an afternoon-to-night shift; fairly tidy (tidiness 0.52); punctual (jitter scale 0.78); rarely forgets pocket items; mood strongly affects behaviour (sensitivity 0.84); sleeps in bedroom_1, works at the bedroom_1 desk. Hobbies: baking, family_calls, journaling, yoga. Chores they take on: grocery_run, vacuuming.
- **Yuki** (resident_2): retired; fairly tidy (tidiness 0.56); average timing (jitter scale 1.16); sometimes forgets pocket items; mood barely affects behaviour (sensitivity 0.29); sleeps in bedroom_1, works at the bedroom_1 desk. Hobbies: baking, guitar. Chores they take on: dusting, fixing, grocery_run, vacuuming.

Pet: a dog, mainly looked after by Yuki.

## Day 7 — Monday

**Active causes**

- `ep:afternoon_slump:resident_2:d7`: Yuki hits an afternoon slump: things get left where they were used for a couple of hours (15:03–17:32).
- `ep:restless_evening:resident_1:d7`: Marco can't settle this evening and wanders between rooms with things in hand (20:39–24:00).
- `ep:tidy_mood:resident_1:d7`: Marco is in a tidying mood for a while: a quick tidy of the living room, and things go back where they belong (10:53–12:53).

Internal states (0–1): Marco energy 0.50, hurriedness 0.32, distraction 0.00; Yuki energy 0.78, hurriedness 0.56, distraction 0.49

**Active causes**

- `ep:craving_a_snack:resident_2:d23`: Yuki is craving a snack and fits one in when there is a gap; the bowl and glass tend to be left out (16:10–16:40).
- `ep:early_night:resident_2:d23`: Yuki turns in early tonight: a short evening, bed 45 minutes sooner (20:30–24:00).

- `ep:craving_a_snack:resident_2:d25`: Yuki is craving a snack and fits one in when there is a gap; the bowl and glass tend to be left out (14:50–15:20).
